## `AFG-0144-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Protein Analyst
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Wool Fineness & Small Ruminant Carcass Quality
- **Intro:** Protein Analyst — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Wool Fineness & Small Ruminant Carcass Quality — Pre-Operational Calibration Verification: pH meter slope: 95-102%; balance accuracy: ±0.1 g; thermometer accuracy: ±0.2°C; microtome section thickness: 5.0 ± 0.5 μm.
